# Seed 32 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - the first run with honest sizes (Iteration 111)

`seed 32 tag2 6h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 10 --cpus 2,8`,
commit 44ddf25bc (Iteration 110: engines linked with -N, no page padding),
started 21:53: four workers (cpus 2, 8, 12, 4), population 256, 98
generations before the deadline, 25,016 designs; 1,377 refused by the reach
limit, no other death. (Seed 31 never ran.) Session 28 measured it with seeds
26, 27, 28 and 30, every design rebuilt under -N (calibration 0.998; cpu 2
0.998, cpu 8 0.997).

**The stopping rule: not small** - the smallest +3.8%.

| objective | seed 32's best | before (seeds 26-30, this session) | improvement | bar |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| fastest | 0.131 (67c070bd47) | 0.133 (seed 30) | +1.5% | 5% |
| smallest binary + image | **22,342** (333b2c6807) | 23,231 (seed 26) | **+3.8%** | 2% |
| least memory | 188 KB | 186 KB | none | 8 KB |

With sizes that are a design's own bytes, the small end moved at once: 889
bytes below anything before - the page padding's lottery had hidden it. The
front of all runs: **111** - 96 of seed 32, 8 of 30, 5 of 28, one each of 26
and 27; 24 by memory alone. The fastest 67c070bd47, 0.131 at 37,408 bytes;
the smallest 333b2c6807, 22,342 bytes (1.204). Q (information): log-range
best 5af912edcb (0.349 at 24,932 bytes, 192 KB); plain product best
447c638144 (0.147 at 33,872).

**jitx with honest sizes** - its 30 clean child/parent pairs: +456 bytes (the
page padding had shown ~4.3 KB), the kernel workload 0.883, the held-out
sieve 0.154 (6.5x), the rest ~1.0; on 18 of the run's 149 front designs, 9 of
the 111. t2wide on 132 of 149, opbody 130, hotinl 115, the JIT 39, rtempty 25.

Its front born late again: 66 of 149 in generations 80-98.

## The run's report

# Evolved VM designs

25016 designs evaluated, 23639 alive. Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, parse, corpus, loop,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
